## 任务要求（上游 3e32c89 #204 本地化）
软超时=「继续后台运行」非终态事件 + 独立硬超时 fail-closed；前端断线/刷新可恢复同一 job，禁止重复提交昂贵分析；软超时、硬超时、真实失败、完成**四态可区分**；复用本地 `_job_timeout_for_config`、持久化报告、SSE、job store；不得把历史 `failed(timeout)` 无条件重写为 running。

## 必须逐项核验（引用文件:行）
1. 软超时后 job 仍运行，且最终**只产生一次** completed/failed（找双写/重复终态路径，含 Redis 与内存 store 两实现）
2. 硬超时 fail-closed；历史 failed(timeout) 不被重写为 running；`_job_timeout_for_config` 是否被复用而非绕过
3. 刷新/断线恢复：恢复路径不会重复创建 job；幂等性证据（测试或代码路径）
4. Redis 与内存 store 语义一致（超时/恢复/终态一次性行为对齐；注意 Redis 不可用时 skip 的测试留下的语义盲区）
5. 前端：SSE 断线重连后状态恢复正确；`job.overtime` 错过时靠轮询 `status.overtime` 补偿的接线是否真的闭环；「后台继续」展示而非失败；jobLifecycle 工具与 store 接线一致
6. 四态可区分：软超时/硬超时/真实失败/完成在 API 响应、job store、前端展示三层的区分字段是否贯通
7. 边界与约束：未动 prompts/、生产 DB、scheduler 时间、强动作门禁、.env.example；api/main.py 存量端点兼容；前端 `npm run build` 兼容
8. 新测试质量：test_upstream081_timeout_recovery.py 11 项是否真实断言上述语义（抽 3-4 个看断言），jobLifecycle.test.ts 同理

## User preferences

- 当自动开发任务的工作区不干净时，用户要求“先运行 git status；若工作区不干净，停止执行并报告 NEEDS_HUMAN，不要覆盖用户或其他 agent 的修改” -> 第一条仓库命令固定为 `git status --short --branch`；存在修改或未跟踪文件即为硬阻断，不读取任务文档、不执行 dry-run、写入或测试步骤。[Task 2][Task 3]
- 用户要求无 ready 任务时“只读检查并提出 1-3 个 proposed 草案，不写代码、不把任务标记为 ready” -> 只进入 REQUEST_TASKS/建议模式；建议生成器输出不等于授权，不擅自修正状态、依赖、数据库污染或 scheduler。[Task 1][Task 2][Task 3]
- 用户要求先阅读 `AGENTS.md`、`docs/AUTO_DEV_PLAN.md`、`docs/TASKS.md`、`docs/DEVLOG.md`，再严格按预定流程执行；这一阅读仅在工作区门禁通过后进行。[Task 1][Task 3]
- 用户明确禁止修改 `tradingagents/prompts/`、写生产 `tradingagents.db`、删除 `logs/` 或 `eval_results/`、全市场扫描、股票深度 TA、调用 DeepSeek、push/PR/merge，以及输出强买卖建议 -> 执行前后检查这些禁区。[Task 3]
- 用户要求自动开发结束输出“选中任务、修改文件、测试、commit、运行档案、风险和下一步建议” -> 结果保留完整审计链，而非只报成功/失败；门禁拦截或无任务时明确无选中任务、无修改、未测试、无 commit/本轮档案及人工下一步。[Task 1][Task 2][Task 3]
- 项目采用 Git/docs 作为多工具事实源；任务状态、依赖、验收命令必须与机器解析器一致。生产数据库、运行产物与潜在密钥文件默认检查并排除。[Task 4]

## Reusable knowledge

- 先运行 `git status --short --branch`；只有工作区干净后才读取指定项目文档并运行 `./scripts/auto_dev_loop.sh --dry-run`。只有 dry-run 选出 ready 任务并获得执行授权，才运行 `./scripts/auto_dev_loop.sh`。[Task 1][Task 2][Task 3]
- 2026-08-19、2026-08-22、2026-08-25、2026-08-28 与 2026-08-29 的干净 checkout dry-run 均输出 `No status=ready tasks found`，因此没有开发、验收测试、commit 或 `docs/task_runs` 档案；只读检查可覆盖任务文档、开发日志、最近提交、任务依赖和 git status。2026-08-25、2026-08-28 与 2026-08-29 的 dry-run 却分别写入 `docs/task_suggestions/2026-08-25.md`、`docs/task_suggestions/2026-08-28.md`、`docs/task_suggestions/2026-08-29.md`，使工作区变脏；此文件仅供人工审核，不代表任务 ready。Related skill: skills/tradingagents-auto-dev-preflight/SKILL.md.[Task 1]
- 建议器与任务真源/状态解析已脱节：较早一轮混入 `V-009` 至 `V-012`，2026-08-25/28 又重提已完成的 `B-001`、`B-002-R1`、`PLAYBOOK-002`。2026-08-29 的最近提交显示 `TA-TUSHARE-2000-001A-R1`、`B-002-R2`、`CONFIG-DS-SCHEDULE-R1` 已人工收口。在 REQUEST_TASKS 模式不得写入任务池或绕过状态门禁；先修复 done/blocked/未满足依赖过滤并以“done 任务不得重新建议”回归测试验证。`AUTO-011-R1`、`AUTO-012-R1`、`AUTO-013` 只是本轮 proposed，仍需人工确认。[Task 1]
- preflight 的 `@test.com` 测试数据库污染和运行中的 scheduler（当时 PID 960，可能触发 LLM）仅是风险信号；先人工确认归因和影响，不自动清理数据或杀进程。[Task 1]
- 自动化记忆写入为环境外部步骤；确认 `$CODEX_HOME` 已设置且可写。若得到 `mkdir: /automations: Read-only file system`，改用绝对路径 `/Users/maybee/.codex/automations/tradingagents-ashare/memory.md`；本机 `python` 不在 PATH 时用 `python3` 或 `date`，但绝不能因此越过仓库门禁。[Task 1][Task 2][Task 3]
- 用 `.venv/bin/python scripts/task_dependency_resolver.py dry-run --tasks-file docs/TASKS.md` 检查依赖图、可领取任务和 auto-release candidates。验收命令必须写在反引号中的真实 `pytest ...`，供解析器填入 `test_cmds`；无命令会退回默认 smoke/full 测试，不能视作任务特定验收。[Task 4]
- 上游 v0.8.1 只能逐提交审计，不能整仓 merge/cherry-pick；`UPSTREAM-081-001` 的 round4 修复提交为 `30e2e96`，任务状态提交 `b2b65ed`，经人工复跑 136 项测试放行后 `UPSTREAM-081-002` 已释放为 ready；`002`→`003`→`004` 仍串行。Codex round4 原始结论是 FAIL（任务状态行混入 diff），不能改写为 Codex 自动 PASS。`UPSTREAM-081-005` Investoday 在 API key、授权、许可、限流和字段口径确认前保持人工确认；换 checkout 必须重验。[Task 4]

## Failures and how to do differently

- 本 rollout 是执行协议而非执行证据：不能声称任务成功、测试通过、产生了 commit 或运行档案；每次从当前 `git status` 和 dry-run 开始并记录验证结果。[Task 3]
- 症状：任务池有 `[ready]` 任务但工作区含修改或未跟踪档案。原因：把任务选择置于工作区所有权之前。修复：立即 `NEEDS_HUMAN`，不读取任务文档、不运行 dry-run 或任何写入/测试；人工处理当前改动后重新触发。[Task 2][Task 3]
- 症状：dry-run 建议包含 done/blocked 任务，或依赖仍指向被取代的 NEEDS_HUMAN 任务。原因：建议器过滤与任务依赖真源不同步。修复：为混合 done/blocked/proposed fixture 增加测试，修复依赖解析/任务文档后再人工确认；不自动标 ready 或绕过依赖。[Task 1]
- 症状：`--dry-run` 在无 ready 任务路径写入 `docs/task_suggestions/...`，令干净 checkout 变脏。原因：dry-run 的建议输出有仓库内副作用。修复：改为只读 stdout 或明确外部/隔离目录；若已写入则立即停止，人工处理该文件并恢复 clean tree 后才可再次自动开发。[Task 1]
- 症状：preflight 发现 `@test.com` 或 scheduler 风险后被自动清理。修复：保留只读告警和 `NEEDS_HUMAN`/人工确认边界；先确认是否为测试数据、进程归属和 LLM 调用影响。[Task 1]

+async def _save_report_or_raise(
+    job_id: str,
+    save_callable: Callable[[], Any],
+    *,
+    stage: str,
+) -> Any:
+    """[UPSTREAM-081-002] Run a report DB finalizer without turning persistence
+    errors into success.
+
+    此前报告保存失败只会打日志，job 随后被标成 completed，出现“无报告的完成”
+    幻觉状态。现在持久化失败会抛回 _run_job_inner 的失败边界，保证“真实失败”
+    与“完成”两态可区分。
+    """
+    try:
+        return await asyncio.to_thread(save_callable)
+    except Exception as exc:
+        message = f"Failed to {stage} report for job {job_id}: {exc}"
+        _log(message)
+        raise RuntimeError(message) from exc

+def test_bool_roundtrip_matches_memory_store(store: RedisJobStore):
+    """[UPSTREAM-081-002] Booleans must round-trip as real bools.
+
+    ``overtime`` is produced by the soft-timeout path and consumed by
+    ``get_job_status`` (``bool(job.get("overtime", False))``). If Redis stored
+    ``str(True) == "True"``, any string encoding would be truthy on read and
+    Redis semantics would diverge from the in-memory store.
+    """
+    store.set_job("j1", status="running", overtime=False, overtime_at=None)
+    store.set_job("j2", status="running", overtime=True, overtime_at="2026-09-07T00:00:00+00:00")
+
+    j1 = store.get_job("j1")
+    j2 = store.get_job("j2")
+
+    # Exact types matter: no "False"/"True" string encoding may survive.
+    assert j1["overtime"] is False
+    assert j2["overtime"] is True
+    assert isinstance(j1["overtime"], bool)
+    assert isinstance(j2["overtime"], bool)
+    # None still round-trips as None for overtime_at.
+    assert j1["overtime_at"] is None
+    assert j2["overtime_at"] == "2026-09-07T00:00:00+00:00"
+
+    # Same writes through the in-memory store must observe identical values.
+    memory = InMemoryJobStore()
+    memory.set_job("j1", status="running", overtime=False, overtime_at=None)
+    memory.set_job("j2", status="running", overtime=True, overtime_at="2026-09-07T00:00:00+00:00")
+    assert memory.get_job("j1")["overtime"] is j1["overtime"]
+    assert memory.get_job("j2")["overtime"] is j2["overtime"]
+    assert memory.get_job("j1")["overtime_at"] == j1["overtime_at"]
+    assert memory.get_job("j2")["overtime_at"] == j2["overtime_at"]
+
+

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""JobStore abstraction for managing analysis job state and SSE events.
     2	
     3	Provides a Protocol defining the interface and an InMemoryJobStore implementation
     4	that replicates the current module-level _jobs / _job_events behavior in api/main.py.
     5	"""
     6	from __future__ import annotations
     7	
     8	import asyncio
     9	import logging
    10	import os
    11	import threading
    12	from datetime import datetime, timezone
    13	from typing import Any, AsyncIterator, Dict, Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable
    14	
    15	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16	
    17	_TERMINAL_STATUSES = frozenset({"completed", "failed"})
    18	_TERMINAL_EVENTS = frozenset({"job.completed", "job.failed"})
    19	
    20	# Per-job event queue cap. Prevents unbounded memory growth when an SSE
    21	# subscriber disconnects but `_run_job_inner` keeps emitting events. When the
    22	# cap is hit, the oldest event is dropped to make room.
    23	_QUEUE_MAXSIZE = int(os.environ.get("JOB_EVENT_QUEUE_MAXSIZE", "2000"))
    24	
    25	# How long to retain completed/failed job state before deleting it. The same
    26	# job_id is fetched by polling clients and the SSE subscriber after the
    27	# terminal event fires, so we can't delete immediately.
    28	_INMEMORY_JOB_TTL = int(os.environ.get("INMEMORY_JOB_TTL", "600"))  # 10 min
    29	
    30	
    31	def _utcnow_iso() -> str:
    32	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    33	
    34	
    35	@runtime_checkable
    36	class JobStore(Protocol):
    37	    """Interface for job state + event storage."""
    38	
    39	    def set_job(self, job_id: str, **fields: Any) -> None:
    40	        """Create or update job fields (merge semantics, thread-safe)."""
    41	        ...
    42	
    43	    def get_job(self, job_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    44	        """Return job fields as dict, or empty dict if not found."""
    45	        ...
    46	
    47	    def delete_job(self, job_id: str) -> None:
    48	        """Remove job state and associated event queue."""
    49	        ...
    50	
    51	    def emit_event(self, job_id: str, event: str, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    52	        """Push SSE event for job (thread-safe, works from both event loop and worker threads)."""
    53	        ...
    54	
    55	    def subscribe(self, job_id: str, *, poll_interval: float = 15.0) -> AsyncIterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
    56	        """Async generator yielding events.
    57	
    58	        On timeout with no events: yield ping if job still running,
    59	        or terminate if completed/failed.
    60	        Terminal events: job.completed, job.failed.
    61	        """
    62	        ...
    63	
    64	    def clear(self) -> None:
    65	        """Reset all state (used on startup)."""
    66	        ...
    67	
    68	
    69	class InMemoryJobStore:
    70	    """In-process job store using threading.Lock and asyncio.Queue.
    71	
    72	    This matches the exact behavior of the module-level _jobs dict,
    73	    _job_events queues, and helper functions in api/main.py.
    74	    """
    75	
    76	    def __init__(self) -> None:
    77	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
    78	        self._jobs: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    79	        self._job_events: Dict[str, asyncio.Queue[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    80	        # Loop captured on first emit/subscribe so worker threads can schedule
    81	        # put_nowait via call_soon_threadsafe even after the original
    82	        # asyncio.get_event_loop() path was deprecated.
    83	        self._loop: Optional[asyncio.AbstractEventLoop] = None
    84	        # Pending TTL cleanup timers, keyed by job_id, so we can cancel/replace
    85	        # them if the job state is updated after the terminal event.
    86	        self._cleanup_handles: Dict[str, asyncio.TimerHandle] = {}
    87	
    88	    def _capture_loop(self) -> Optional[asyncio.AbstractEventLoop]:
    89	        loop, _ = self._resolve_loop()
    90	        return loop
    91	
    92	    def _resolve_loop(self) -> "tuple[Optional[asyncio.AbstractEventLoop], bool]":
    93	        """Return (loop, on_loop). ``loop`` is the running loop if available,
    94	        else the cached one captured by a previous call. ``on_loop`` is True
    95	        only when the caller is currently executing on the event loop thread.
    96	        """
    97	        try:
    98	            running = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    99	        except RuntimeError:
   100	            return self._loop, False
   101	        # First-time capture is racy across threads; only set once to keep
   102	        # all worker threads pointing at the same loop.
   103	        with self._lock:
   104	            if self._loop is None:
   105	                self._loop = running
   106	        return running, True
   107	
   108	    # ── state management ────────────────────────────────────────────────
   109	
   110	    def set_job(self, job_id: str, **fields: Any) -> None:
   111	        with self._lock:
   112	            if job_id not in self._jobs:
   113	                self._jobs[job_id] = {}
   114	            self._jobs[job_id].update(fields)
   115	            new_status = fields.get("status")
   116	        # Adjust TTL cleanup based on the new status:
   117	        #   - moving INTO completed/failed → arm a cleanup timer
   118	        #   - moving OUT of terminal (e.g. rerun, status="running") → cancel
   119	        #     any pending timer so we don't drop a freshly-restarted job
   120	        if new_status in _TERMINAL_STATUSES:
   121	            self._schedule_cleanup(job_id)
   122	        elif new_status is not None:
   123	            self._cancel_cleanup(job_id)
   124	
   125	    def get_job(self, job_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   126	        with self._lock:
   127	            return dict(self._jobs.get(job_id, {}))
   128	
   129	    def delete_job(self, job_id: str) -> None:
   130	        with self._lock:
   131	            self._jobs.pop(job_id, None)
   132	            self._job_events.pop(job_id, None)
   133	            handle = self._cleanup_handles.pop(job_id, None)
   134	        if handle is not None:
   135	            handle.cancel()
   136	
   137	    def _cancel_cleanup(self, job_id: str) -> None:
   138	        with self._lock:
   139	            handle = self._cleanup_handles.pop(job_id, None)
   140	        if handle is not None:
   141	            handle.cancel()
   142	
   143	    def _schedule_cleanup(self, job_id: str) -> None:
   144	        """Drop job state and event queue after _INMEMORY_JOB_TTL seconds."""
   145	        loop, on_loop = self._resolve_loop()
   146	        if loop is None or not loop.is_running():
   147	            # No event loop available (e.g. called from a worker thread before
   148	            # the loop was captured). The next subscribe()/emit_event() will
   149	            # try again; in the worst case the entry leaks until process
   150	            # restart, which is the pre-fix behavior.
   151	            return
   152	
   153	        def _do_cleanup() -> None:
   154	            with self._lock:
   155	                self._jobs.pop(job_id, None)
   156	                self._job_events.pop(job_id, None)
   157	                self._cleanup_handles.pop(job_id, None)
   158	
   159	        with self._lock:
   160	            existing = self._cleanup_handles.pop(job_id, None)
   161	        if existing is not None:
   162	            existing.cancel()
   163	
   164	        def _arm() -> None:
   165	            handle = loop.call_later(_INMEMORY_JOB_TTL, _do_cleanup)
   166	            with self._lock:
   167	                self._cleanup_handles[job_id] = handle
   168	
   169	        if on_loop:
   170	            _arm()
   171	        else:
   172	            try:
   173	                loop.call_soon_threadsafe(_arm)
   174	            except RuntimeError:
   175	                # Loop closed mid-call (e.g. during process shutdown). Don't
   176	                # let a cleanup-arming failure propagate up and abort the
   177	                # status write that triggered us.
   178	                logger.debug("Could not arm cleanup for %s: loop closed", job_id)
   179	
   180	    # ── event queue ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  2651	async def _run_job(
  2652	    job_id: str,
  2653	    request: AnalyzeRequest,
  2654	    stream_events: bool = False,
  2655	    save_report: bool = True,
  2656	    user_id: Optional[str] = None,
  2657	    request_source: str = "api",
  2658	) -> None:
  2659	    # [UPSTREAM-081-002] 软/硬超时双门。
  2660	    # 软超时（_resolve_job_timeout → _job_timeout_for_config，按用户配置区分
  2661	    # 普通通道与智谱 Coding 通道）到达后只发非终态 job.overtime 事件，任务继续
  2662	    # 在后台运行，最终仍只会产生一次 completed/failed。
  2663	    # 硬超时（_JOB_HARD_TIMEOUT）是独立的 fail-closed 兜底：到达后 cancel 内部
  2664	    # 协程并标记 failed，释放调度槽位，避免僵尸任务永久占用线程池。
  2665	    # 内层任务放在独立 task 中运行，跨软超时不 cancel to_thread 工作；
  2666	    # 硬超时 cancel 协程，阻止其恢复执行后续的报告写入与终态写。
  2667	    job_timeout = _resolve_job_timeout(request, user_id)
  2668	    started_monotonic = time.monotonic()
  2669	    inner_task = asyncio.create_task(
  2670	        _run_job_inner(job_id, request, stream_events, save_report, user_id, request_source)
  2671	    )
  2672	    try:
  2673	        if job_timeout > 0:
  2674	            soft_wait_seconds = job_timeout
  2675	            if _JOB_HARD_TIMEOUT > 0:
  2676	                soft_wait_seconds = min(soft_wait_seconds, _JOB_HARD_TIMEOUT)
  2677	            done, _ = await asyncio.wait({inner_task}, timeout=soft_wait_seconds)
  2678	            if inner_task not in done and not inner_task.done():
  2679	                # 硬上限不大于软上限时不发误导性的加班提示，直接落到下方硬超时处理。
  2680	                if _JOB_HARD_TIMEOUT <= 0 or job_timeout < _JOB_HARD_TIMEOUT:
  2681	                    overtime_at = _utcnow_iso()
  2682	                    elapsed_seconds = time.monotonic() - started_monotonic
  2683	                    message = (
  2684	                        f"分析耗时较长（已超过 {job_timeout} 秒），后台仍在继续，"
  2685	                        "正在等待最终结果，请勿重复提交。"
  2686	                    )
  2687	                    _log(f"[Job {job_id}] {message}")
  2688	                    _set_job(
  2689	                        job_id,
  2690	                        status="running",
  2691	                        overtime=True,
  2692	                        overtime_at=overtime_at,
  2693	                        error=None,
  2694	                    )
  2695	                    _emit_job_event(
  2696	                        job_id,
  2697	                        "job.overtime",
  2698	                        {
  2699	                            "job_id": job_id,
  2700	                            "elapsed_seconds": elapsed_seconds,
  2701	                            "soft_timeout_seconds": job_timeout,
  2702	                            "overtime_at": overtime_at,
  2703	                            "message": message,
  2704	                        },
  2705	                    )
  2706	
  2707	        if _JOB_HARD_TIMEOUT > 0 and not inner_task.done():
  2708	            remaining_seconds = max(
  2709	                0.0,
  2710	                _JOB_HARD_TIMEOUT - (time.monotonic() - started_monotonic),
  2711	            )
  2712	            done, _ = await asyncio.wait({inner_task}, timeout=remaining_seconds)
  2713	            if inner_task not in done and not inner_task.done() and inner_task.cancel():
  2714	                try:
  2715	                    await inner_task
  2716	                except asyncio.CancelledError:
  2717	                    pass
  2718	
  2719	                elapsed_seconds = time.monotonic() - started_monotonic
  2720	                err_msg = (
  2721	                    f"任务达到硬性运行上限（{_JOB_HARD_TIMEOUT} 秒），已终止。"
  2722	                    "请检查模型或数据源的请求超时配置后重试。"
  2723	                )
  2724	                _log(f"[Job {job_id}] {err_msg}")
  2725	                _set_job(
  2726	                    job_id,
  2727	                    status="failed",
  2728	                    error=err_msg,
  2729	                    overtime=False,
  2730	                    overtime_at=None,
  2731	                    finished_at=_utcnow_iso(),
  2732	                )
  2733	                # 注意：不能用 asyncio.to_thread 写 DB，因为线程池可能被僵尸任务占满导致死锁。
  2734	                # 用同步方式直接写，SQLite 的写入足够快不会阻塞事件循环。

  5538	def _require_job_owner(job_id: str, current_user: UserDB) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  5539	    job = _get_job(job_id)
  5540	    if not job:
  5541	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="job not found")
  5542	    owner_id = job.get("user_id")
  5543	    if owner_id and owner_id != current_user.id:
  5544	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="job not found")
  5545	    return job

     2	
     3	参考上游 3e32c89（#204），验收口径：
     4	
     5	1. 软超时（_resolve_job_timeout → _job_timeout_for_config）是非终态事件：
     6	   job 保持 ``status="running"`` 且 ``overtime=True``，后台任务继续运行，
     7	   最终只产生一次 completed/failed 终态（防双写）。
     8	2. 硬超时（TA_JOB_HARD_TIMEOUT）独立 fail-closed：cancel 内层协程、标记
     9	   failed、调用 ``report_service.mark_report_failed``，四态中的“硬超时”
    10	   文案可区分。
    11	3. 历史 ``failed(timeout)`` 记录读路径不得被改写为 running（恢复轮询据此
    12	   继续等待或收口，但不无条件重写状态）。
    13	4. 报告持久化失败不得伪装成 completed（_save_report_or_raise 抛回失败边界）。

    34	class _RecordingJobStore(InMemoryJobStore):
    35	    """InMemoryJobStore that records emitted events in order."""
    36	
    37	    def __init__(self) -> None:
    38	        super().__init__()
    39	        self.events: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
    40	        self._event_hooks: dict[str,callable] = {}
    41	
    42	    def emit_event(self, job_id: str, event: str, data: dict) -> None:
    43	        self.events.append((event, data))
    44	        hook = self._event_hooks.get(event)
    45	        if hook is not None:
    46	            hook()
    47	        super().emit_event(job_id, event, data)
    48	
    49	    def event_names(self) -> list[str]:
    50	        return [name for name, _ in self.events]

    81	def _wedged_inner(
    82	    release: asyncio.Event,
    83	    *,
    84	    on_start=None,
    85	    on_release=None,
    86	    cancelled_flag: list[bool] | None = None,
    87	):
    88	    """Build a _run_job_inner replacement that blocks until release."""
    89	
    90	    async def _inner(job_id, request, stream_events=False, save_report=True,
    91	                     user_id=None, request_source="api"):
    92	        try:
    93	            if on_start is not None:
    94	                on_start(job_id)
    95	            await release.wait()
    96	            if on_release is not None:
    97	                on_release(job_id)
    98	        except asyncio.CancelledError:
    99	            if cancelled_flag is not None:
   100	                cancelled_flag.append(True)
   101	            raise
   102	        return None
   103	
   104	    return _inner

   117	def test_soft_timeout_keeps_job_running_then_completes_once(
   118	    store: _RecordingJobStore,
   119	    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
   120	    no_db_failure_mark,
   121	):
   122	    """软超时后 job 仍 running + overtime=True；内层完成后只有一次 completed。"""
   123	    release = asyncio.Event()
   124	    job_id = "job-soft-ok"
   125	
   126	    def _on_start(jid: str) -> None:
   127	        api_main._set_job(jid, status="running")
   128	
   129	    def _on_release(jid: str) -> None:
   130	        # 内层工作流在软超时后于后台完成：只写一次终态
   131	        api_main._set_job(
   132	            jid,
   133	            status="completed",
   134	            decision="HOLD",
   135	            error=None,
   136	            overtime=False,
   137	            overtime_at=None,
   138	            finished_at=api_main._utcnow_iso(),
   139	        )
   140	        api_main._emit_job_event(jid, "job.completed", {"job_id": jid, "decision": "HOLD"})
   141	
   142	    # 软超时事件到达后释放内层任务，让它在后台继续完成
   143	    store._event_hooks["job.overtime"] = release.set
   144	
   145	    monkeypatch.setattr(
   146	        api_main, "_run_job_inner",
   147	        _wedged_inner(release, on_start=_on_start, on_release=_on_release),
   148	    )
   149	    _patch_timeouts(monkeypatch, soft=0.05, hard=30.0)
   150	
   151	    asyncio.run(_run_job_async(job_id))
   152	
   153	    job = store.get_job(job_id)
   154	    assert job["status"] == "completed"
   155	    assert job["overtime"] is False
   156	    assert job["overtime_at"] is None
   157	    assert job["error"] is None
   158	
   159	    names = store.event_names()
   160	    # 软超时提示恰好一次；终态恰好一次；绝不出现 job.failed
   161	    assert names.count("job.overtime") == 1
   162	    assert names.count("job.completed") == 1
   163	    assert "job.failed" not in names
   164	    # 防双写：终态事件必须是最后一个
   165	    assert names[-1] == "job.completed"
   166	    no_db_failure_mark.assert_not_called()

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Redis-backed JobStore implementation.
     2	
     3	Uses Redis Hash for job state and Pub/Sub for real-time SSE event delivery,
     4	enabling multi-worker API deployment where all uvicorn workers share the same
     5	job state and event stream.
     6	"""
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import asyncio
    10	import json
    11	import logging
    12	import os
    13	import threading
    14	from datetime import datetime, timezone
    15	from typing import Any, AsyncIterator, Dict
    16	
    17	import redis
    18	
    19	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    20	
    21	# TTL for job state hashes (seconds). Configurable via env var.
    22	_JOB_STATE_TTL: int = int(os.environ.get("JOB_STATE_TTL", "86400"))
    23	
    24	_TERMINAL_EVENTS = frozenset({"job.completed", "job.failed"})
    25	
    26	
    27	def _utcnow_iso() -> str:
    28	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    29	
    30	
    31	def _serialize_value(v: Any) -> str:
    32	    """Serialize a Python value for storage in a Redis Hash field."""
    33	    if v is None:
    34	        return ""
    35	    # [UPSTREAM-081-002] Booleans must be JSON-encoded: str(True) == "True"
    36	    # would round-trip back as the string "True", making overtime=False in
    37	    # Redis differ from overtime=False in the in-memory store (bool("False")
    38	    # is also truthy, so any string encoding of booleans is unsafe).
    39	    if isinstance(v, (dict, list, bool)):
    40	        return json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False)
    41	    return str(v)
    42	
    43	
    44	def _deserialize_value(v: str) -> Any:
    45	    """Attempt to deserialize a Redis Hash field value back to Python."""
    46	    if v == "":
    47	        return None
    48	    # Try JSON first (covers dicts, lists, and JSON-encoded scalars)
    49	    try:
    50	        return json.loads(v)
    51	    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):
    52	        return v
    53	
    54	
    55	class RedisJobStore:
    56	    """Redis-backed job store using Hash for state and Pub/Sub for events.
    57	
    58	    Key schema (all prefixed with *prefix*):
    59	        {prefix}job:{job_id}      -- Redis Hash holding job fields
    60	        {prefix}events:{job_id}   -- Pub/Sub channel for SSE events
    61	    """
    62	
    63	    def __init__(self, redis_url: str, prefix: str = "ta:") -> None:
    64	        self._prefix = prefix
    65	        self._r: redis.Redis = redis.Redis.from_url(
    66	            redis_url, decode_responses=True
    67	        )
    68	        # Verify connectivity once at creation
    69	        self._r.ping()
    70	        logger.info("RedisJobStore connected to %s (prefix=%r)", redis_url, prefix)
    71	
    72	    # ── key helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    73	
    74	    def _job_key(self, job_id: str) -> str:
    75	        return f"{self._prefix}job:{job_id}"
    76	
    77	    def _channel_key(self, job_id: str) -> str:
    78	        return f"{self._prefix}events:{job_id}"
    79	
    80	    # ── state management ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    81	
    82	    def set_job(self, job_id: str, **fields: Any) -> None:
    83	        """Create or update job fields (merge semantics).
    84	
    85	        Complex values and booleans are JSON-serialized; None becomes empty string.
    86	        Each call refreshes the TTL.
    87	        """
    88	        if not fields:
    89	            return
    90	        key = self._job_key(job_id)
    91	        mapping = {k: _serialize_value(v) for k, v in fields.items()}
    92	        pipe = self._r.pipeline()
    93	        pipe.hset(key, mapping=mapping)
    94	        pipe.expire(key, _JOB_STATE_TTL)
    95	        pipe.execute()
    96	
    97	    def get_job(self, job_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    98	        """Return job fields as dict, or empty dict if not found."""
    99	        raw = self._r.hgetall(self._job_key(job_id))
   100	        if not raw:
   101	            return {}
   102	        return {k: _deserialize_value(v) for k, v in raw.items()}
   103	
   104	    def delete_job(self, job_id: str) -> None:
   105	        """Remove job state hash. Deleting a non-existent key is a no-op."""
   106	        self._r.delete(self._job_key(job_id))
   107	
   108	    # ── event pub/sub ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
   109	
   110	    def emit_event(self, job_id: str, event: str, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   111	        """Publish an SSE event payload on the job's Pub/Sub channel."""
   112	        payload: Dict[str, Any] = {
   113	            "event": event,
   114	            "data": data,
   115	            "timestamp": _utcnow_iso(),
   116	        }
   117	        self._r.publish(self._channel_key(job_id), json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False))
   118	
   119	    async def subscribe(
   120	        self, job_id: str, *, poll_interval: float = 15.0
   121	    ) -> AsyncIterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
   122	        """Async generator yielding events for *job_id*.
   123	
   124	        Internally spins up a background thread running the blocking Redis
   125	        Pub/Sub listener and bridges messages into an asyncio.Queue via
   126	        call_soon_threadsafe.
   127	
   128	        On timeout with no events:
   129	          - If job is still running, yield a ping event.
   130	          - If job is completed/failed, terminate the generator.
   131	        On terminal events (job.completed, job.failed), yield and terminate.
   132	        """
   133	        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
   134	        queue: asyncio.Queue[Dict[str, Any]] = asyncio.Queue()
   135	        stop_event = threading.Event()
   136	
   137	        def _listener() -> None:
   138	            """Blocking listener running in a daemon thread."""
   139	            pubsub = self._r.pubsub()
   140	            try:

     1	// [UPSTREAM-081-002] 长时分析软/硬超时与断线恢复的纯函数工具。
     2	// 软超时（job.overtime）是非终态事件：任务仍在后台运行，最终只会产生一次
     3	// completed/failed。这里集中维护“事件 → 运行状态”的映射与历史
     4	// failed(timeout) 记录的归类，禁止把任意 failed 无条件当作 running。
     5	
     6	export type AnalysisRunState = 'idle' | 'running' | 'completed' | 'failed'
     7	
     8	export interface JobLifecycleUpdate {
     9	    isAnalyzing: boolean
    10	    runState: AnalysisRunState
    11	    overtimeNotice: string | null
    12	}
    13	
    14	export type RecoveredJobDisposition = 'running' | 'completed' | 'failed'
    15	
    16	export const DEFAULT_OVERTIME_NOTICE = '分析耗时较长，后台仍在继续，正在等待最终结果，请勿重复提交。'
    17	export const RECOVERY_POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 3000
    18	// 断线恢复最多轮询 2 小时（与后端硬超时上限同量级），超限后停止等待。
    19	export const RECOVERY_POLL_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 2 * 60 * 60 / (RECOVERY_POLL_INTERVAL_MS / 1000)
    20	export const RECOVERY_POLL_TIMEOUT_MESSAGE = '已停止等待任务状态。后端任务可能仍在处理，请稍后到历史报告查看最终结果。'
    21	// 历史失败文案：旧版外层看门狗把软超时标成 failed（“任务超时（超过 N 秒），
    22	// 已自动终止”），但内层分析可能仍在运行并最终落盘报告。
    23	export const LEGACY_SOFT_TIMEOUT_PATTERN = /任务超时（超过\s*\d+\s*秒）/
    24	
    25	export function hasRecoveryPollingReachedLimit(
    26	    attempts: number,
    27	    maxAttempts: number = RECOVERY_POLL_MAX_ATTEMPTS,
    28	): boolean {
    29	    return attempts >= maxAttempts
    30	}
    31	
    32	export function getJobLifecycleUpdate(
    33	    eventName: string,
    34	    data: Record<string, unknown> = {},
    35	): JobLifecycleUpdate | null {
    36	    switch (eventName) {
    37	        case 'job.running':
    38	            return { isAnalyzing: true, runState: 'running', overtimeNotice: null }
    39	        case 'job.overtime': {
    40	            const suppliedMessage = data.message ?? data.msg
    41	            return {
    42	                isAnalyzing: true,
    43	                runState: 'running',
    44	                overtimeNotice: typeof suppliedMessage === 'string' && suppliedMessage.trim()
    45	                    ? suppliedMessage
    46	                    : DEFAULT_OVERTIME_NOTICE,
    47	            }
    48	        }
    49	        case 'job.completed':
    50	            return { isAnalyzing: false, runState: 'completed', overtimeNotice: null }
    51	        case 'job.failed':
    52	            if (classifyRecoveredJobStatus('failed', typeof data.error === 'string' ? data.error : null) === 'running') {
    53	                // 历史软超时文案不代表终态：保持“后台继续”，等待真正的完成事件。
    54	                return {
    55	                    isAnalyzing: true,
    56	                    runState: 'running',
    57	                    overtimeNotice: DEFAULT_OVERTIME_NOTICE,
    58	                }
    59	            }
    60	            return { isAnalyzing: false, runState: 'failed', overtimeNotice: null }
    61	        default:
    62	            return null
    63	    }
    64	}
    65	
    66	export function classifyRecoveredJobStatus(status: string, error?: string | null): RecoveredJobDisposition {
    67	    if (status === 'completed') return 'completed'
    68	    if (status !== 'failed') return 'running'
    69	
    70	    // 旧策略把软超时写成 failed(timeout)，但内层工作流可能仍在运行并能落盘
    71	    // completed 报告。只有匹配该历史文案时才当作 running 继续等待；
    72	    // 硬超时（“硬性运行上限”）与真实失败必须保持 failed，不做无条件改写。
    73	    if (LEGACY_SOFT_TIMEOUT_PATTERN.test(error || '')) return 'running'
    74	    return 'failed'
    75	}

     3	    classifyRecoveredJobStatus,

     5	    getJobLifecycleUpdate,
     6	    hasRecoveryPollingReachedLimit,
     7	} from './jobLifecycle'

     9	describe('classifyRecoveredJobStatus', () => {
    10	    it('maps completed to completed', () => {
    11	        expect(classifyRecoveredJobStatus('completed')).toBe('completed')
    12	    })
    13	
    14	    it('maps pending/running to running', () => {
    15	        expect(classifyRecoveredJobStatus('running')).toBe('running')
    16	        expect(classifyRecoveredJobStatus('pending')).toBe('running')
    17	    })
    18	
    19	    it('keeps real failures as failed', () => {
    20	        expect(classifyRecoveredJobStatus('failed', '模型 API Key 无效')).toBe('failed')
    21	        expect(classifyRecoveredJobStatus('failed', null)).toBe('failed')
    22	        expect(classifyRecoveredJobStatus('failed', '')).toBe('failed')
    23	    })
    24	
    25	    it('keeps hard-timeout failures as failed', () => {
    26	        expect(
    27	            classifyRecoveredJobStatus('failed', '任务达到硬性运行上限（7200 秒），已终止。请检查模型或数据源的请求超时配置后重试。'),
    28	        ).toBe('failed')
    29	    })
    30	
    31	    it('treats legacy soft-timeout failures as still running', () => {
    32	        expect(

    34	        ).toBe('running')
    35	        expect(
    36	            classifyRecoveredJobStatus('failed', '任务超时（超过 2700 秒），已自动终止'),
    37	        ).toBe('running')
    38	    })
    39	
    40	    it('never rewrites arbitrary timeout-ish errors to running', () => {
    41	        expect(classifyRecoveredJobStatus('failed', 'Request timed out after 1800 ms')).toBe('failed')
    42	        expect(classifyRecoveredJobStatus('failed', '任务超时')).toBe('failed')
    43	    })
    44	})

   349	def test_redis_bool_serialization_matches_memory_store():
   350	    """overtime 布尔经 Redis 序列化后必须还原为真实布尔，与内存 store 一致。
   351	
   352	    get_job_status 用 bool(job.get("overtime", False)) 读取；若 Redis 以
   353	    str(True)="True" 存储，任何字符串读回都是真值，两边语义就会分裂。
   354	    """
   355	    from api.job_store_redis import _deserialize_value, _serialize_value
   356	
   357	    for value in (False, True):
   358	        raw = _serialize_value(value)
   359	        restored = _deserialize_value(raw)
   360	        assert isinstance(restored, bool)
   361	        assert restored is value
   362	        # 与内存 store 的原生布尔完全一致
   363	        memory = InMemoryJobStore()
   364	        memory.set_job("j", overtime=value)
   365	        assert memory.get_job("j")["overtime"] is restored
   366	
   367	    # None / 常规标量语义保持不变
   368	    assert _deserialize_value(_serialize_value(None)) is None

   207	        if (signal.aborted) {
   208	            resolve(false)
   209	            return
   210	        }
   211	        const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
   212	            signal.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort)
   213	            resolve(true)
   214	        }, ms)
   215	        const onAbort = () => {
   216	            window.clearTimeout(timer)
   217	            resolve(false)
   218	        }
   219	        signal.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true })
   220	    })

   228	        pushSystem(`分析流中断，正在回查任务状态：${currentJobId}`)
   229	        setIsConnected(false)
   230	        setIsAnalyzing(true)
   231	        setAnalysisRunState('running')
   232	
   233	        while (!signal.aborted) {
   234	            if (hasRecoveryPollingReachedLimit(recoveryPollAttempts)) {
   235	                pushAssistant(RECOVERY_POLL_TIMEOUT_MESSAGE)
   236	                setCurrentHorizon(null)
   237	                setIsAnalyzing(false)
   238	                setAnalysisRunState('failed', RECOVERY_POLL_TIMEOUT_MESSAGE)
   239	                return true
   240	            }
   241	            recoveryPollAttempts += 1
   242	
   243	            try {
   244	                const status = await api.getJobStatus(currentJobId)
   245	                if (signal.aborted) return false
   246	                const disposition = classifyRecoveredJobStatus(status.status, status.error)
   247	
   248	                if (disposition === 'completed') {
   249	                    const result = await api.getJobResult(currentJobId)
   250	                    if (signal.aborted) return false
   251	                    setReport(result.result)
   252	
   253	                    const symbol = result.result.symbol
   254	                    const tradeDate = result.result.trade_date
   255	                    if (symbol) {
   256	                        setCurrentSymbol(symbol)
   257	                        onSymbolDetected(symbol)
   258	                    }
   259	
   260	                    try {
   300	                if (signal.aborted) return false
   301	                // 任务记录不存在（服务重启清空了内存 store / 超过 TTL）：
   302	                // 停止回查并按失败收口，避免无限轮询；不自动重新提交。
   303	                const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
   304	                if (/job not found|404/i.test(message)) {
   305	                    finalizeFailedRun('任务状态已失效（服务端无此任务记录），请到历史报告中查看是否已生成报告。')
   306	                    pushAssistant('任务状态已失效（服务端无此任务记录）。为避免重复消耗，未自动重新发起分析；请到历史报告中查看。')
   307	                    return true
   308	                }
   309	                console.warn('任务状态回查失败，将继续重试:', error)
   310	            }
   311	
   312	            if (!await sleepUntilRetry(RECOVERY_POLL_INTERVAL_MS, signal)) return false
   313	        }
   314	
   315	        return false
   316	    }
   317	
   318	    useEffect(() => {
   319	        const container = messagesContainerRef.current
   320	        if (!container) return
   321	        container.scrollTo({
   322	            top: container.scrollHeight,
   323	            behavior: 'smooth',
   324	        })
   325	    }, [chatMessages])
   326	
   327	    // [UPSTREAM-081-002] 卸载时中止回查，避免离开页面后继续轮询。
   328	    useEffect(() => () => {
   329	        recoveryAbortRef.current?.abort()
   330	    }, [])
   331	
   332	    // [UPSTREAM-081-002] 刷新恢复：挂载时若存在仍在运行的 job，回查同一任务
   333	    // 而不是让用户重新提交。running 态下 isAnalyzing=true 已阻止重复提交。
   334	    const mountRecoveryRanRef = useRef(false)
   335	    useEffect(() => {
   336	        if (mountRecoveryRanRef.current) return
   337	        mountRecoveryRanRef.current = true
   338	        const { currentJobId, analysisRunState } = useAnalysisStore.getState()
   339	        if (!currentJobId || analysisRunState !== 'running') return
   340	        const controller = new AbortController()
   341	        recoveryAbortRef.current = controller
   342	        void recoverInterruptedJob(controller.signal).finally(() => {
   343	            if (recoveryAbortRef.current === controller) {
   344	                recoveryAbortRef.current = null
   345	            }
   346	        })
   347	        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   348	    }, [])
   349	
   350	    const pushAssistant = (content: string) => {
   351	        addChatMessage({
   352	            id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,
   353	            role: 'assistant',
   354	            content,
   355	            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
   356	        })
   357	    }
   358	
   359	    const pushSystem = (content: string) => {
   360	        addChatMessage({
   361	            id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,
   362	            role: 'system',
   363	            content,
   364	            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
   365	        })
   366	    }
   367	
   368	    const clearTypingIndicator = () => {
   369	        if (!typingIndicatorIdRef.current) return
   370	        const id = typingIndicatorIdRef.current
   371	        useAnalysisStore.setState(state => ({
   372	            chatMessages: state.chatMessages.filter(m => m.id !== id)
   373	        }))
   374	        typingIndicatorIdRef.current = null
   375	    }
   376	
   377	    const finalizeFailedRun = (message: string) => {
   378	        clearTypingIndicator()
   379	        pendingAgentMsgIdsRef.current = new Set()
   380	        forceUpdate(n => n + 1)
   381	        markAgentMessagesComplete()
   382	        failRun(message)
   383	    }
   384	
   385	    const parseAndDispatch = (event: StreamEvent) => {
   386	        const { event: eventName, data } = event
   387	        // [UPSTREAM-081-002] 运行态统一由生命周期映射维护，软超时不再误判为失败
   388	        const lifecycleUpdate = getJobLifecycleUpdate(eventName, data)
   389	        if (lifecycleUpdate) {
   390	            setIsAnalyzing(lifecycleUpdate.isAnalyzing)
   391	            setAnalysisRunState(
   392	                lifecycleUpdate.runState,
   393	                eventName === 'job.failed' ? String(data.error || 'unknown error') : null,
   394	            )
   395	            setAnalysisOvertimeNotice(lifecycleUpdate.overtimeNotice)
   396	        }
   397	
   398	        switch (eventName) {
   399	            case 'job.ready':
   400	                setIsConnected(true)
   401	                if (data.job_id) setCurrentJobId(String(data.job_id))
   402	                // 把 typing indicator 换成"解析中"提示，告知用户正在识别标的
   403	                if (typingIndicatorIdRef.current) {
   404	                    setMessageContent(typingIndicatorIdRef.current, '__parsing__')
   405	                }
   406	                break
   407	            case 'job.created': {
   408	                const jobId = String(data.job_id || '')
   409	                const symbol = String(data.symbol || '')
   410	                if (jobId) setCurrentJobId(jobId)
   411	                if (symbol) {
   412	                    setCurrentSymbol(symbol)
   413	                    onSymbolDetected(symbol)
   414	                }
   415	                // 切换 indicator 到"采集数据"阶段
   416	                if (typingIndicatorIdRef.current) {
   417	                    setMessageContent(typingIndicatorIdRef.current, `__status:collecting:${symbol}__`)
   418	                }
   419	                streamingReportIds.current.clear()
   420	                agentMessageMapRef.current = {}
   421	                firstTokenMapRef.current = {}
   422	                sectionToMsgIdsRef.current = {}
   423	                pendingAgentMsgIdsRef.current = new Set(); forceUpdate(n => n + 1)
   424	                break
   425	            }
   426	            case 'job.running':
   427	                // 运行态已由 lifecycle hook 统一设置
   428	                // 切换 indicator 到"分析启动"阶段
   429	                if (typingIndicatorIdRef.current) {
   430	                    setMessageContent(typingIndicatorIdRef.current, '__status:analyzing__')
   620	                // 气泡已经表示 agent 正在撰写，不再额外发系统消息
   621	                break
   622	            case 'agent.milestone': {
   623	                const { stage, title, summary } = data as { stage: string; title: string; summary: string }
   624	                if (stage === 'final_decision') {
   625	                    pushAssistant(`**${title}**\n\n${summary}`)
   626	                }
   627	                break
   628	            }
   629	            case 'agent.debate.token': {
   630	                const raw = data as Record<string, unknown>
   631	                const debate = raw.debate
   632	                const token = raw.token
   633	                if (
   634	                    (debate !== 'research' && debate !== 'risk') ||
   635	                    typeof raw.agent !== 'string' ||
   636	                    typeof raw.round !== 'number' ||
   637	                    typeof token !== 'string'
   638	                ) break
   639	                appendDebateToken(
   640	                    debate, raw.agent, raw.round, token,
   641	                    typeof raw.horizon === 'string' ? raw.horizon : undefined,
   642	                )
   643	                break
   644	            }
   645	            case 'agent.debate': {
   646	                const raw = data as Record<string, unknown>
   647	                const debate = raw.debate
   648	                const agent = raw.agent
   649	                const round = raw.round
   650	                const content = raw.content
   651	                if (
   652	                    (debate !== 'research' && debate !== 'risk') ||
   653	                    typeof agent !== 'string' ||
   654	                    typeof round !== 'number' ||
   655	                    typeof content !== 'string'
   656	                ) {
   657	                    console.warn('[SSE] Malformed agent.debate payload, skipping:', raw)
   658	                    break
   659	                }
   660	                addDebateMessage({
   661	                    debate,
   662	                    agent,
   663	                    round,
   664	                    content,
   665	                    isVerdict: raw.is_verdict === true,
   666	                    horizon: typeof raw.horizon === 'string' ? raw.horizon : undefined,
   667	                })
   668	                break
   669	            }
   670	            default:
   671	                break
   672	        }
   673	    }
   674	
   675	    const streamChat = async (prompt: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<boolean> => {
   676	        // [TA-UI-001] analysis_console_horizon_intent - build user context from props
   677	        const objectiveMap: Record<string, string> = {
   678	            watch: '观察',
   679	            entry: '建仓/入场研究',
   680	            holding: '持仓复盘',
   681	            add: '加仓判断',
   682	            reduce: '减仓/止损',
   683	        }
   684	        const horizonLabel = horizon === 'medium' ? '中线' : '短线'
   685	        const effectiveObjective = intent ? objectiveMap[intent] : undefined
   686	        const effectiveHorizon = horizon === 'medium' ? '中线' : '短线'
   687	        const userContext = {
   688	            objective: effectiveObjective,
   689	            investment_horizon: effectiveHorizon,
   690	        }
   691	        // Prepend structured context to prompt so intent parser sees it
   692	        const contextPrefix = `[分析上下文] 周期=${horizonLabel}｜意图=${objectiveMap[intent || 'watch'] || '观察'}｜持仓=${hasPositionProp ? '是' : '否'}\n`
   693	        const fullPrompt = contextPrefix + prompt
   694	        const response = await api.chatCompletion(
   695	            [{ role: 'user', content: fullPrompt }],
   696	            true,
   697	            selectedAnalysts,
   698	            signal,
   699	            userContext,
   700	            fullTaConfirmed  // [PERF-004] full_ta_cost_gate
   701	                ? { runtime_tier: 'FULL_TA', confirmed_full_ta: true, runtime_profile: 'FULL_TA' }
   702	                : undefined,
   703	        )
   704	
   705	        if (!response.body) throw new Error('SSE stream unavailable')
   706	
   707	        const reader = response.body.getReader()
   708	        const decoder = new TextDecoder()
   709	        let buffer = ''
   710	        let currentEvent = 'message'
   711	
   712	        while (true) {
   713	            const { value, done } = await reader.read()
   714	            if (done) break
   715	            buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true })
   716	
   717	            const blocks = buffer.split('\n\n')
   718	            buffer = blocks.pop() || ''
   719	
   720	            for (const block of blocks) {
   721	                const lines = block.split('\n')
   722	                let dataLine = ''
   723	                for (const raw of lines) {
   724	                    const line = raw.trim()
   725	                    if (!line) continue
   726	                    if (line.startsWith('event:')) currentEvent = line.slice(6).trim()
   727	                    else if (line.startsWith('data:')) dataLine = line.slice(5).trim()
   728	                }
   729	
   730	                if (!dataLine) continue
   731	                if (dataLine === '[DONE]' || currentEvent === 'done') {
   732	                    setIsConnected(false)
   733	                    // [UPSTREAM-081-002] 流结束≠任务结束：若未见终态事件，
   734	                    // 保持运行态并交由回查流程收口，不在此关闭 analyzing。
   735	                    return terminalEventSeenRef.current
   736	                }
   737	                
   738	                if (currentEvent === 'ping') {
   739	                    continue
   740	                }
   741	
   742	                try {
   743	                    const data = JSON.parse(dataLine) as Record<string, unknown>
   744	                    parseAndDispatch({ event: currentEvent, data })
   745	                } catch {
   746	                    console.error('SSE解析失败:', dataLine.slice(0, 120))
   747	                }
   748	            }
   749	        }
   750	
   751	        setIsConnected(false)
   752	        return terminalEventSeenRef.current
   753	    }
   754	
   755	    const handleSubmit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
   756	        e.preventDefault()
   757	        const prompt = input.trim()
   758	        // [UPSTREAM-081-002] isAnalyzing（含软超时加班与回查等待）时禁止重复提交昂贵分析
   759	        if (!prompt || streaming || isAnalyzing) return
   760	
   761	        // Inject custom analysis prompt from settings if set
   762	        const customPrompt = localStorage.getItem('ta-custom-prompt')?.trim() || ''
   763	        const fullPrompt = customPrompt ? `${prompt}\n\n[分析要求] ${customPrompt}` : prompt
   764	
   765	        setInput('')
   766	        addChatMessage({
   767	            id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,
   768	            role: 'user',
   769	            content: prompt,
   770	            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
   771	        })
   772	
   773	        reset()
   774	        streamingReportIds.current.clear()
   775	        pendingAgentMsgIdsRef.current = new Set(); forceUpdate(n => n + 1)
   776	
   777	        // 立刻插入 typing indicator，让用户知道系统正在响应
   778	        const typingId = `typing-${Date.now()}`
   779	        typingIndicatorIdRef.current = typingId
   780	        addChatMessage({
   781	            id: typingId,
   782	            role: 'assistant',
   783	            content: '__typing__',
   784	            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
   785	        })
   786	
   787	        setStreaming(true)
   788	        setIsAnalyzing(true)
   789	        setIsConnected(false)
   790	        setAnalysisRunState('running')
   791	        terminalEventSeenRef.current = false
   792	        const abortController = new AbortController()
   793	        abortControllerRef.current = abortController
   794	        stopHandledRef.current = false
   795	        recoveryAbortRef.current?.abort()
   796	        const recoveryController = new AbortController()
   797	        recoveryAbortRef.current = recoveryController
   798	
   799	        try {
   800	            const sawTerminalEvent = await streamChat(fullPrompt, abortController.signal)
   801	            if (!sawTerminalEvent && !abortController.signal.aborted) {
   802	                // 流断了但后端任务可能仍在跑：回查同一个 job，不重复提交
   803	                const recovered = await recoverInterruptedJob(recoveryController.signal)
   804	                if (!recovered && !recoveryController.signal.aborted) {
   805	                    finalizeFailedRun('分析流在任务编号返回前中断')
   806	                    pushAssistant('请求中断：未能取得任务编号，无法继续回查任务状态。')
   807	                }
   808	            }
   809	        } catch (error) {
   810	            // 出错时清理 typing indicator
   811	            clearTypingIndicator()
   812	            const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'unknown error'
   813	            if (error instanceof DOMException && error.name === 'AbortError') {
   814	                if (stopHandledRef.current) return
   815	                finalizeFailedRun('用户已停止本次分析')
   816	                pushAssistant('已停止本次分析。')
   817	                return
   818	            }
   819	            const canRecover = Boolean(useAnalysisStore.getState().currentJobId)
   820	            if (canRecover) {
   821	                const recovered = await recoverInterruptedJob(recoveryController.signal)
   822	                if (!recovered && !recoveryController.signal.aborted) {
   823	                    finalizeFailedRun(errorMessage)
   824	                    pushAssistant(`请求中断：${errorMessage}\n\n后端任务可能仍在执行，请稍后到历史报告中查看结果。`)
   825	                }
   826	            } else {
   827	                finalizeFailedRun(errorMessage)
   828	                pushAssistant(`请求失败：${errorMessage}`)
   829	            }
   830	        } finally {
   831	            if (abortControllerRef.current === abortController) {
   832	                abortControllerRef.current = null
   833	            }
   834	            if (recoveryAbortRef.current === recoveryController) {
   835	                recoveryAbortRef.current = null
   836	            }
   837	            setStreaming(false)
   838	        }
   839	    }
   840	
   925	            {/* 快速提示 */}
   926	            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-3">
   927	                {PRESET_PROMPTS.map((prompt) => (
   928	                    <button
   929	                        key={prompt}
   930	                        onClick={() => setInput(prompt)}
   931	                        className="text-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-600 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 hover:border-blue-400 dark:hover:border-blue-500 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-slate-200"
   932	                    >
   933	                        {prompt}
   934	                    </button>
   935	                ))}
   936	            </div>
   937	
   938	            {/* [UPSTREAM-081-002] 软超时提示：后台继续，非失败态 */}
   939	            {analysisOvertimeNotice && (
   940	                <div
   941	                    role="status"
   942	                    className="mb-3 flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-amber-300 bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-amber-800 dark:border-amber-500/40 dark:bg-amber-500/10 dark:text-amber-300"
   943	                >
   944	                    <Loader2 className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 animate-spin" />
   945	                    <span>{analysisOvertimeNotice}</span>
   946	                </div>
   947	            )}
   948	
   949	            {/* 聊天内容 */}
   950	            <div ref={messagesContainerRef} className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 pr-1">
   951	                {chatMessages.map((msg) => {
   952	                    // Report card
   953	                    if (msg.role === 'report' && msg.section) {
   954	                        return (
   955	                            <ReportCard
   956	                                key={msg.id}
   957	                                section={msg.section}
   958	                                content={msg.content}
   959	                                streaming={!msg.complete}
   960	                                onOpen={() => onShowReport?.(msg.section)}
   961	                            />
   962	                        )
   963	                    }
   964	
   965	                    // Status indicator（提交后立即显示，随 SSE 事件切换阶段）
   966	                    if (msg.content.startsWith('__')) {
   967	                        const c = msg.content
   968	                        let label = ''
   969	                        let icon: 'dots' | 'spin' = 'dots'
   970	                        let colorCls = 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-500'
   971	
   972	                        if (c === '__typing__') {
   973	                            label = ''
   974	                            icon = 'dots'
   975	                        } else if (c === '__parsing__') {
   976	                            label = '正在识别标的与意图...'
   977	                            icon = 'spin'
   978	                            colorCls = 'bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-500/10 border-blue-200 dark:border-blue-500/30 text-blue-500 dark:text-blue-400'
   979	                        } else if (c.startsWith('__status:collecting:')) {
   980	                            const sym = c.replace('__status:collecting:', '').replace('__', '')
   981	                            label = `已识别 ${sym}，正在采集行情数据...`
   982	                            icon = 'spin'
   983	                            colorCls = 'bg-cyan-50 dark:bg-cyan-500/10 border-cyan-200 dark:border-cyan-500/30 text-cyan-500 dark:text-cyan-400'
   984	                        } else if (c === '__status:analyzing__') {
   985	                            label = '数据就绪，多智能体协作分析启动中...'
   986	                            icon = 'spin'
   987	                            colorCls = 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-500 dark:text-emerald-400'
   988	                        }
   989	
   990	                        return (
   991	                            <div key={msg.id} className="flex items-center gap-2">
   992	                                <div className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs transition-colors duration-300 ${colorCls}`}>
   993	                                    {icon === 'spin' ? (
   994	                                        <>
   995	                                            <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin shrink-0" />
   996	                                            <span className="animate-pulse">{label}</span>
   997	                                        </>
   998	                                    ) : (
   999	                                        <>
  1000	                                            <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-current animate-bounce" style={{ animationDelay: '0ms' }} />
  1001	                                            <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-current animate-bounce" style={{ animationDelay: '150ms' }} />
  1002	                                            <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-current animate-bounce" style={{ animationDelay: '300ms' }} />
  1003	                                        </>
  1004	                                    )}
  1005	                                </div>
  1006	                            </div>
  1007	                        )
  1008	                    }
  1009	
  1010	                    // Agent streaming messages → compact card with live preview
  1011	                    const agentMeta = msg.agent ? AGENT_META_MAP[msg.agent] : null
  1012	                    const isPending = pendingAgentMsgIdsRef.current.has(msg.id)
  1013	                    const isCompleted = !!msg.complete
  1014	                    const isExpanded = expandedAgentMsgId === msg.id
  1015	
  1016	                    if (msg.agent && agentMeta && msg.role === 'assistant') {
  1017	                        // Extract preview text: strip markdown headers, bold, collapse whitespace
  1018	                        const textOnly = msg.content
  1019	                            .replace(/^#{1,4}\s+.*$/gm, '')
  1020	                            .replace(/\*\*/g, '')
  1021	                            .replace(/\n{2,}/g, ' ')
  1022	                            .trim()
  1023	                        const preview = textOnly.slice(0, 80)
  1024	
  1025	                        // 已完成的 agent 卡片 → 和 ReportCard 视觉统一
  1026	                        if (isCompleted) {
  1027	                            return (
  1028	                                <div key={msg.id} className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/50 overflow-hidden transition-all">
  1029	                                    <button
  1030	                                        onClick={() => setExpandedAgentMsgId(prev => prev === msg.id ? null : msg.id)}
  1031	                                        className="w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2.5 text-left hover:border-blue-400 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors group"
  1032	                                    >
  1033	                                        <span className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center w-7 h-7 rounded-lg ${agentMeta.bgCls} shrink-0`}>
  1034	                                            <agentMeta.Icon className={`w-4 h-4 ${agentMeta.iconCls}`} />
  1035	                                        </span>
  1036	                                        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">

   182	    def _ensure_queue(self, job_id: str) -> asyncio.Queue[Dict[str, Any]]:
   183	        with self._lock:
   184	            q = self._job_events.get(job_id)
   185	            if q is None:
   186	                q = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=_QUEUE_MAXSIZE)
   187	                self._job_events[job_id] = q
   188	            return q
   189	
   190	    @staticmethod
   191	    def _put_with_overflow(q: "asyncio.Queue[Dict[str, Any]]", payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   192	        """Push payload, dropping the oldest event if the queue is full.
   193	
   194	        Bounded queues prevent runaway memory growth when an SSE subscriber
   195	        disconnects but `_run_job_inner` keeps emitting events.
   196	        """
   197	        try:
   198	            q.put_nowait(payload)
   199	        except asyncio.QueueFull:
   200	            try:
   201	                q.get_nowait()
   202	            except asyncio.QueueEmpty:
   203	                pass
   204	            try:
   205	                q.put_nowait(payload)
   206	            except asyncio.QueueFull:
   207	                # Extremely contended; drop the new event rather than block.
   208	                pass
   209	
   210	    def emit_event(self, job_id: str, event: str, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   211	        """Thread-safe event emitter.
   212	
   213	        Uses put_nowait directly when called from the event loop, and
   214	        call_soon_threadsafe when called from a worker thread. The queue is
   215	        bounded; oldest events are dropped on overflow so a stalled SSE
   216	        consumer cannot exhaust process memory.
   217	        """
   218	        payload: Dict[str, Any] = {
   219	            "event": event,
   220	            "data": data,
   221	            "timestamp": _utcnow_iso(),
   222	        }
   223	        q = self._ensure_queue(job_id)
   224	        loop, on_loop = self._resolve_loop()
   225	
   226	        if on_loop:
   227	            self._put_with_overflow(q, payload)
   228	        elif loop is not None and loop.is_running():
   229	            try:
   230	                loop.call_soon_threadsafe(self._put_with_overflow, q, payload)
   231	            except RuntimeError:
   232	                # Loop closed; fall through to direct put.
   233	                self._put_with_overflow(q, payload)
   234	        else:
   235	            # Best-effort fallback: just push directly. The SSE consumer
   236	            # on the loop side will pick it up on the next wait_for cycle.
   237	            self._put_with_overflow(q, payload)
   238	
   239	    async def subscribe(
   240	        self, job_id: str, *, poll_interval: float = 15.0
   241	    ) -> AsyncIterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
   242	        """Async generator yielding events for *job_id*.
   243	
   244	        On timeout:
   245	          - If job is still running, yield a ping event.
   246	          - If job is completed or failed, replay a terminal event and terminate.
   247	        On terminal events (job.completed, job.failed), yield and terminate.
   248	        When the generator exits (terminal event or client disconnect), the
   249	        backing event queue is dropped to free memory.
   250	        """
   251	        self._capture_loop()
   252	        q = self._ensure_queue(job_id)
   253	        try:
   254	            while True:
   255	                try:
   256	                    event = await asyncio.wait_for(q.get(), timeout=poll_interval)
   257	                    yield event
   258	                    if event["event"] in ("job.completed", "job.failed"):
   259	                        break
   260	                except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   261	                    with self._lock:
   262	                        job = dict(self._jobs.get(job_id, {}))
   263	                        status = job.get("status")
   264	                    if status in ("completed", "failed"):
   265	                        if status == "completed":
   266	                            yield {
   267	                                "event": "job.completed",
   268	                                "data": {
   269	                                    "job_id": job_id,
   270	                                    "decision": job.get("decision"),
   271	                                    "direction": job.get("direction") or (job.get("result") or {}).get("direction"),
   272	                                    "result": job.get("result"),
   273	                                    "risk_items": job.get("risk_items") or [],
   274	                                    "key_metrics": job.get("key_metrics") or [],
   275	                                    "confidence": job.get("confidence") or (job.get("result") or {}).get("confidence"),
   276	                                    "target_price": job.get("target_price") or (job.get("result") or {}).get("target_price"),
   277	                                    "stop_loss_price": job.get("stop_loss_price") or (job.get("result") or {}).get("stop_loss_price"),
   278	                                },
   279	                                "timestamp": _utcnow_iso(),
   280	                            }

   145	                        try:
   146	                            payload = json.loads(msg["data"])
   147	                        except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
   148	                            continue
   149	                        loop.call_soon_threadsafe(queue.put_nowait, payload)
   150	            except Exception:
   151	                logger.debug("Redis pubsub listener for %s exiting", job_id, exc_info=True)
   152	            finally:
   153	                try:
   154	                    pubsub.unsubscribe()
   155	                    pubsub.close()
   156	                except Exception:
   157	                    pass
   158	
   159	        thread = threading.Thread(target=_listener, daemon=True)
   160	        thread.start()
   161	
   162	        try:
   163	            while True:
   164	                try:
   165	                    event = await asyncio.wait_for(queue.get(), timeout=poll_interval)
   166	                    yield event
   167	                    if event["event"] in _TERMINAL_EVENTS:
   168	                        break
   169	                except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   170	                    status = self.get_job(job_id).get("status")
   171	                    if status in ("completed", "failed"):
   172	                        break
   173	                    yield {
   174	                        "event": "ping",
   175	                        "data": {"timestamp": _utcnow_iso()},
   176	                        "timestamp": _utcnow_iso(),
   177	                    }
   178	        finally:
   179	            stop_event.set()
   180	            thread.join(timeout=3.0)
   181	
   182	    # ── lifecycle ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   183	
   184	    def clear(self) -> None:
   185	        """Delete all job state keys matching this store's prefix.
   186	
   187	        Uses SCAN to avoid blocking the Redis server with KEYS.
   188	        """
   189	        cursor = 0
   190	        pattern = f"{self._prefix}job:*"
   191	        while True:
   192	            cursor, keys = self._r.scan(cursor=cursor, match=pattern, count=200)
   193	            if keys:
   194	                self._r.delete(*keys)
   195	            if cursor == 0:
   196	                break

  4965	_RESOLVABLE_SYMBOL_RE = re.compile(
  4966	    r"^("
  4967	    r"\d{6}\.(SH|SZ|BJ)"          # A 股 / 北交所
  4968	    r"|\d{4,5}\.HK"                # 港股
  4969	    r"|[A-Z][A-Z0-9]{0,10}"         # 美股 ticker（可含数字）
  4970	    r"|[A-Z][A-Z0-9]{0,5}-[A-Z0-9]"  # 美股类股 ticker
  4971	    r"|[A-Z][A-Z0-9]{0,5}\.[A-Z0-9]{1,3}"  # 显式美股交易所后缀
  4972	    r")$"
  4973	)

  4975	
  4976	def _has_consistent_cn_exchange(symbol: str) -> bool:
  4977	    """Reject CN codes whose explicit exchange suffix contradicts the code."""
  4978	    if _is_cn_index_symbol(str(symbol or "")):
  4979	        return True
  4980	    match = re.fullmatch(r"(\d{6})\.(SH|SZ|BJ)", str(symbol or "").upper())
  4981	    if not match:
  4982	        return True
  4983	    code, suffix = match.groups()
  4984	    if code.startswith(("4", "8", "920")):
  4985	        expected = "BJ"
  4986	    elif code.startswith(("5", "6", "9")):
  4987	        expected = "SH"
  4988	    else:
  4989	        expected = "SZ"
  4990	    return suffix == expected
  4991	
  4992	
  4993	def _is_valid_analysis_symbol(symbol: str) -> bool:
  4994	    return bool(
  4995	        _RESOLVABLE_SYMBOL_RE.fullmatch(str(symbol or ""))
  4996	        and _has_consistent_cn_exchange(symbol)
  4997	    )
  4998	
  4999	
  5000	def _normalize_analysis_symbol(raw: str) -> str:
  5001	    """Normalize an analysis symbol without truncating a valid generic ticker."""
  5002	    candidate = str(raw or "").strip().upper()
  5003	    candidate = re.sub(r"\.SS$", ".SH", candidate)
  5004	    prefixed_cn = re.fullmatch(r"(SH|SZ|BJ)(\d{6})", candidate)
  5005	    if prefixed_cn:
  5006	        exchange, code = prefixed_cn.groups()
  5007	        normalized = f"{code}.{exchange}"
  5008	        return normalized if _is_valid_analysis_symbol(normalized) else candidate
  5009	    if _is_valid_analysis_symbol(candidate):
  5010	        return candidate
  5011	    if re.fullmatch(r"\d{6}", candidate):
  5012	        return _normalize_symbol(candidate)
  5013	    # Malformed explicit symbols must be rejected by the validator, never
  5014	    # truncated by the permissive legacy normalizer (1234567 -> 123456.SZ).
  5015	    return candidate
  5016	
  5017	
  5018	def _resolve_analysis_symbol(raw: str) -> str:
  5019	    """Resolve a direct analysis target without fuzzy company-name guesses."""
  5020	    normalized = _normalize_analysis_symbol(raw)
  5021	    if _is_valid_analysis_symbol(normalized):
  5022	        return normalized
  5023	
  5024	    candidate = str(raw or "").strip()
  5025	    if not re.search(r"[\u4e00-\u9fff]", candidate):
  5026	        return normalized
  5027	    mapped = _load_cn_stock_map().get(candidate)
  5028	    if not mapped:
  5029	        return normalized
  5030	    resolved = _normalize_analysis_symbol(mapped)
  5031	    return resolved if _is_valid_analysis_symbol(resolved) else normalized

  5162	def _normalize_ths_code(code: str) -> str:
  5163	    """Convert THS/XQ code like SH601xxx → 601xxx.SH"""
  5164	    code = str(code).strip()
  5165	    if code.upper().startswith("SH"):
  5166	        return f"{code[2:]}.SH"
  5167	    if code.upper().startswith("SZ"):
  5168	        return f"{code[2:]}.SZ"
  5169	    if code.upper().startswith("BJ") or code.upper().startswith("NQ"):
  5170	        return f"{code[2:]}.BJ"
  5171	    # Bare 6-digit code — guess exchange
  5172	    if code.startswith(("6", "5")):
  5173	        return f"{code}.SH"
  5174	    if code.startswith(("0", "3", "2")):
  5175	        return f"{code}.SZ"
  5176	    return code

  5179	@app.get("/v1/market/hot-stocks")
  5180	def get_hot_stocks(source: str = "em", limit: int = 30) -> Dict:
  5181	    """Return hot A-share stocks from different sources.
  5182	    
  5183	    Args:
  5184	        source: Data source selection
  5185	            - 'em': 东方财富热榜 (EastMoney hot stocks)
  5186	            - 'xq': 雪球热门 (Xueqiu most-followed stocks)
  5187	            - 'ths': 连涨榜 (Consecutive rising stocks, not general hot list)
  5188	        limit: Maximum number of stocks to return
  5189	    
  5190	    Returns:
  5191	        Dict with stocks list, total count, source info, and fallback status
  5192	    """
  5193	    import akshare as ak
  5194	
  5195	    # 定义数据源尝试顺序（如果主数据源失败，自动尝试备用源）
  5196	    source_configs = {
  5197	        "em": ("stock_hot_rank_em", None, "东方财富热榜"),
  5198	        "xq": ("stock_hot_follow_xq", "最热门", "雪球热门"),
  5199	        "ths": ("stock_rank_lxsz_ths", None, "连涨榜"),
  5200	    }
  5201	
  5202	    if source not in source_configs:
  5203	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Unknown source: {source}")
  5204	
  5205	    # 尝试主数据源，失败则尝试其他源
  5206	    sources_to_try = [source] + [s for s in ["xq", "em", "ths"] if s != source]
  5207	    last_error = None
  5208	
  5209	    for src in sources_to_try:
  5210	        try:
  5211	            func_name, param, desc = source_configs[src]
  5212	            func = getattr(ak, func_name)
  5213	
  5214	            # 调用 akshare 函数
  5215	            if param:
  5216	                df = func(symbol=param).head(limit)
  5217	            else:
  5218	                df = func().head(limit)
  5219	
  5220	            stocks = []
  5221	
  5222	            if src == "em":
  5223	                for i, (_, row) in enumerate(df.iterrows()):
  5224	                    stocks.append({
  5225	                        "rank": i + 1,
  5226	                        "symbol": _normalize_ths_code(str(row.get("代码", ""))),
  5227	                        "name": str(row.get("股票名称", "")),
  5228	                        "price": float(row.get("最新价", 0) or 0),
  5229	                        "change": float(row.get("涨跌额", 0) or 0),
  5230	                        "change_pct": float(row.get("涨跌幅", 0) or 0),
  5231	                        "extra": "",
  5232	                    })
  5233	
  5234	            elif src == "xq":
  5235	                for i, (_, row) in enumerate(df.iterrows()):
  5236	                    stocks.append({
  5237	                        "rank": i + 1,
  5238	                        "symbol": _normalize_ths_code(str(row.get("股票代码", ""))),
  5239	                        "name": str(row.get("股票简称", "")),
  5240	                        "price": float(row.get("最新价", 0) or 0),
  5241	                        "change": 0.0,
  5242	                        "change_pct": 0.0,
  5243	                        "extra": f"关注 {int(row.get('关注', 0)):,}",
  5244	                    })
  5245	
  5246	            elif src == "ths":
  5247	                for i, (_, row) in enumerate(df.iterrows()):
  5248	                    days = int(row.get("连涨天数", 0) or 0)
  5249	                    change_pct = float(row.get("连续涨跌幅", 0) or 0)
  5250	                    stocks.append({
  5251	                        "rank": i + 1,
  5252	                        "symbol": _normalize_ths_code(str(row.get("股票代码", ""))),
  5253	                        "name": str(row.get("股票简称", "")),
  5254	                        "price": float(row.get("收盘价", 0) or 0),
  5255	                        "change": 0.0,
  5256	                        "change_pct": change_pct,
  5257	                        "extra": f"连涨{days}天",
  5258	                    })
  5259	
  5260	            # 成功获取数据
  5261	            fallback_msg = f" (fallback from {source_configs[source][2]})" if src != source else ""
  5262	            _log(f"Hot stocks: successfully fetched from {desc}{fallback_msg}")
  5263	            return {
  5264	                "stocks": stocks,
  5265	                "total": len(stocks),
  5266	                "source": src,
  5267	                "requested_source": source,
  5268	                "fallback": src != source,
  5269	            }
  5270	
  5271	        except Exception as e:
  5272	            last_error = e
  5273	            _log(f"Hot stocks: {desc} failed - {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:100]}")
  5274	            continue
  5275	
  5276	    # 所有数据源都失败
  5277	    raise HTTPException(
  5278	        status_code=503,
  5279	        detail=f"All data sources failed. Last error: {type(last_error).__name__}: {str(last_error)[:200]}"
  5280	    )
  5281

  5317	@app.post("/v1/analyze", response_model=AnalyzeResponse)

  5335	    # A direct request may omit symbol when the query contains an unambiguous
  5336	    # ticker or A-share/fund name. Resolve it before saved-context lookup and
  5337	    # job creation so reports, holdings and cache keys never use an empty or

  5340	    query_date: Optional[str] = None

  5342	        request.symbol = await asyncio.to_thread(
  5343	            _resolve_analysis_symbol,
  5344	            request.symbol,
  5345	        )
  5346	    if request.query:
  5347	        if await asyncio.to_thread(
  5348	            _query_has_multiple_explicit_instruments,
  5349	            request.query,
  5350	        ):
  5351	            raise HTTPException(
  5352	                status_code=422,
  5353	                detail="一次分析只支持一个明确标的，请拆分多标的请求。",
  5354	            )
  5355	        if request.symbol:
  5356	            query_symbol, query_date = await asyncio.to_thread(
  5357	                _resolve_query_symbol_and_date,
  5358	                request.query,
  5359	            )
  5360	            if query_symbol is None and _query_has_explicit_cn_name_code_pair(

  5362	            ):
  5363	                raise HTTPException(
  5364	                    status_code=422,
  5365	                    detail=(

  5367	                    ),
  5368	                )
  5369	            if query_symbol is None and _query_contains_instrument_code(
  5370	                request.query
  5371	            ):
  5372	                raise HTTPException(
  5373	                    status_code=422,
  5374	                    detail="查询文本中的代码无法确认，请只保留一个明确标的。",
  5375	                )
  5376	            if (
  5377	                query_symbol
  5378	                and _normalize_analysis_symbol(query_symbol)
  5379	                != _normalize_analysis_symbol(request.symbol)
  5380	            ):
  5381	                raise HTTPException(

  5398	        )
  5399	    request.symbol = _normalize_analysis_symbol(request.symbol)
  5400	    if not _is_valid_analysis_symbol(request.symbol):
  5401	        raise HTTPException(
  5402	            status_code=422,
  5403	            detail="分析标的格式无效，请使用 600519.SH、0700.HK 或 AAPL 等明确格式。",
  5404	        )

  5406	        symbol=request.symbol,
  5407	        current_trade_date=request.trade_date,

  5409	        trade_date_was_explicit=trade_date_was_explicit,

  5416	        )
  5417	
  5418	    explicit_context = _extract_request_user_context(request)
  5419

  5422	        resolved_tier = RuntimeTier(tier_value)
  5423	    except ValueError:

  5425

  5452	
  5453	    # Don't block the event loop on a sync SQLite read while the scheduler

  5456	    _apply_user_context_to_request(request, merged_user_context)

  5482	    elif resolved_profile is None:
